% PARAMETERS - Adjust for spacing/sizing

\def\cellsize{0.18}

\def\xorsize{0.35}

\begin{document}

    scale=1,
    block/.style={draw, minimum size=\cellsize cm, inner sep=0pt, outer sep=0pt},
    active/.style={block, fill=\activecolor},
    inactive/.style={block, fill=\inactivecolor},
    arrow/.style={\arrowthickness, -Stealth},
    xor/.style={draw, circle, minimum size=\xorsize cm, inner sep=0pt, font=\tiny, fill=white},

    label/.style={font=\tiny},
    seclabel/.style={font=\small\bfseries},
]
